matrix and per-part Done clauses govern when each part may start and complete.

There are FIVE freeze gates: `s-common`, `s-2d`, `s-3d`, `s-wsi` and `s-3d-ext`. The first four
cover the core; only separately activated `f-ext` waits for the fifth. There is no `s-scene`: the
CORE scene specification is included in `s-3d`, while `SCENE3D_EXTENDED_1.md` is frozen exclusively
by `s-3d-ext`. No core completion condition requires the Extended document.

The shared freeze requirements are ONE ANSWER, SEMANTIC HASH, MINIMA, TOLERANCES and THE GATE
AGREES, defined in the per-part Done section. Numeric constants and algorithms still to be selected
are explicit specification deliverables in `s`; an implementation may not start against an unresolved
alternative. `b`, `c`, `e` and `f` can complete headlessly. `d`, `g` and `i` require `a-wsi` directly;
`h` inherits it through `g` and also consumes `f` and `c` for its retained-layer and 2D/3D tests.

- [x] **Definition of Done for specifications.** The per-part Done list declares each freeze
  independently and includes the five requirements above. Extended work remains separately gated.
- [x] **Split specification freeze.** The `s` table, prerequisite matrix, `f-ext` header and Done
  list all name the five freezes; core Scene3D belongs to `s-3d`, Extended Scene3D to `s-3d-ext`.
- [x] **One DrawList contract.** `b` requires immutable, versioned, cached in-process replay and
  internal encoding round-trip; cross-process `render2d-wire` remains an extension.
- [x] **Remove backend-local specification choices.** `s-3d` chooses deterministic depth and hazard
  rules; `e` implements those frozen rules instead of independently choosing alternatives.
- [x] **Cover every filter.** `c` tests every FilterGraph node, representative compositions and
  backdrop-dependency propagation; the obsolete three-filter restriction is removed.
- [x] **Valid image semantics.** `a-common` uses tagged `ImageSemantics`; alpha and color space live
  only in Color, and `s-common` freezes the storage/semantics/operations matrix.
- [x] **Owned non-color images.** `OwnedImage` owns any valid single-plane, single-sample semantics,
  including masks/data; specialized Render3D attachment resources remain separate.
- [x] **Shared format ownership.** `graphics-core::PixelFormat` includes R32_UINT and RGBA32F storage;
  `render3d` wraps common formats and its specialized depth/stencil formats with a capability table.
- [x] **Explicit image conversion.** `e` requires resolve/conversion when samples, storage, alpha or
  color space differ; sharing image types does not promise zero conversion for incompatible targets.
- [x] **Both core registries.** `h` and its Done clause independently cover Render3D Core and Scene3D
  Core. Extended feature coverage remains in `f-ext`.
- [x] **Three byte spans.** `a-common` separates minimum_visible_bytes, backend_access_span and actual
  allocation_len. CPU views use the minimum; present imports validate the full backend span.
- [x] **WSI memory visibility.** `a-wsi` specifies send-release/receive-acquire in both directions,
  coherent CPU-visible v1 imports and tri-architecture visibility checks; `s-wsi` freezes it.
- [x] **Initialization and padding.** `a-common` requires zero allocation, inter-Domain clearing and
  canonical reserved/padding bytes on export/readback. Full damage is not an initialization proof.
- [x] **Atomic surface configuration.** `a-wsi` specifies snapshots, serial acknowledgment,
  generation invalidation and Full first presentation, with separate close/completion events.
- [x] **Pre-compositor surfaces.** `a-wsi` permits one visible scanout-bound surface; `d` tests
  visibility switching, hidden acquisition refusal and independent queues/lifetimes, not composition.
- [x] **Normative YUV.** `s-common` covers plane layout, ranges, siting, filters, matrices, ordering,
  HDR metadata and malformed-plane refusal before `a-common` implements MultiPlaneImage.
- [x] **Prepared-list invalidation.** `b` binds prepared state to profile/backend/target/resource and
  glyph/filter dependencies while separating mutable image content from immutable geometry/layout.
- [x] **Cheap property changes.** `b` requires a bounded reusable builder with allocation-free
  re-recording within reserved capacity and explicit scratch-growth outcomes; no retained UI is added.
- [x] **Submission and readback.** `e` exposes already-ready or pending completion through one bounded
  API, owns resource lifetime/cancellation rules and is frozen by `s-3d`; `g` implements it.
- [x] **Strict position computation.** `e` and SHADER_IR_1 include all contributing data/control
  dependencies, including transcendentals, and gate implementation on a fully defined ClipCoordQ.
- [x] **Conformance thresholds.** `s` assigns numerical tolerances, image masks and comparison rules
  to each owning profile before its freeze. Extended-only thresholds cannot block a core freeze.
- [x] **Semantic hashing.** `s` requires SHA-256 over canonical machine-readable semantic inputs,
  with a document manifest, explicit encoding/version rules and no raw-Markdown or self-hash cycle.
- [x] **Enforceable soft2d floor.** `c` owns the headless four-scene benchmark, fixed 16.7/66.7 ms
  ceilings and frozen budgets. `d` reuses those fixtures and requires the same benchmark gate;
  `c` does not depend on the live application or WSI to measure its performance.
